Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any reactions module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 773. The projective resolution has length at most 989 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by reactions generators. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 989. QED.
